Use when the user wants a speech-to-speech, S2S, V2V or GPT Live voice demo from one prompt. Stops before writing anything when speech-to-speech is not enabled. - **cold-reviewer**: Zero-context code reviewer. Reviews changes cold - no spec, no project knowledge, no domain context. Catches what familiarity blinds you to. Delegate when you want a fresh-eyes pass before merging, or as part of a multi-perspective review. ([krzysztofsurdy/code-virtuoso](https://github.com/krzysztofsurdy/code-virtuoso/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-reviewer.md) · ★27)
- **cold-reviewer**: Cold diff review by ref. Resolves a commit SHA or base branch without intent framing, cites every finding as severity/claim/file:line, writes an incremental report, and never edits source. Use after gates on a behavior-bearing diff. ([ray-manaloto/dotfiles-legacy](https://github.com/ray-manaloto/dotfiles-legacy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer.md) · ★5)
- **cold-reviewer**: ../../agents/cold-reviewer.md ([p5ych0/watch-pr-skill](https://github.com/p5ych0/watch-pr-skill/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **cold-reviewer**: Reads this branch's changes against the pull request's base cold, the way Codex and Copilot will, and reports what they would raise. Use before the first review request of a pull request and after each round's fixes. Read-only by instruction; it posts nothing. ([p5ych0/watch-pr-skill](https://github.com/p5ych0/watch-pr-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **cold-reviewer**: Performs cold-context single-plan critique when no separate requirements/spec file is available. Infers the problem from the plan itself and surfaces unchallenged assumptions, alternatives, and load-bearing bets. Use only when /review-plan determines no spec file exists. ([DavidRoyBlue/Autodidact](https://github.com/DavidRoyBlue/Autodidact/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **cold-reviewer**: Cold review of a pull request for /work-ticket, round 1 and the verify rounds after it. Fresh context, refutes by default, reports findings and edits nothing. ([matldupont/slipway](https://github.com/matldupont/slipway/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer.md))
- **cold-reviewer**: Review one generated Taku workspace using only local evidence ([Taku-OS/taku-publisher](https://github.com/Taku-OS/taku-publisher/blob/HEAD/packages/repo-to-stax-converter/benchmark/agents/kimi-cold-reviewer.md))
- **cold-reviewer**: Cold review of a PR or commit range it did not write, and re-review of the fix commits when resumed. Reports findings; never edits, commits or comments. ([tarka1939/My_Site](https://github.com/tarka1939/My_Site/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-reviewer.md))

- **cold-scorer**: Adversarial cold-context evaluator. Spawn ONLY with (a) the diff or output artifact, (b) the manifest of what was claimed done, (c) test/lint/exit-code artifacts. Never give it the parent's conversation, reasoning, or excuses. Its output is the only valid source for ledger failure entries and skill-quality scores. ([ktg-one/AiA](https://github.com/ktg-one/AiA/blob/HEAD/sia-loop/agents/cold-scorer.md))
- **cold-solver**: Blind checker for Economics Academy content. Give it ONE temp file path and a mode - "solve" (stems + options, no answers: returns a letter and one-line reasoning per question), "verify" (flashcards + the notes page each must match: returns AGREE/DISAGREE per card), or "tag" (past-paper questions + a taxonomy excerpt: returns topic slugs per question). It never sees the answer key, the source data or a review sheet, so its result is an independent second opinion. ([eliotking01/economics-academy](https://github.com/eliotking01/economics-academy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-solver.md))
- **cold-start-auditor**: Run this agent periodically, or after any significant debugging session, to verify that the repo's knowledge record is lossless. It simulates an agent with zero conversational context — only the repo — and attempts to answer the questions a future maintainer would need answered. Failures identify exactly which knowledge is trapped in conversations or memory rather than in the durable record. ([clyde-tressler/semantic-compactor](https://github.com/clyde-tressler/semantic-compactor/blob/HEAD/agents/cold-start-auditor.md))
- **cold-start-author**: Usability test of austere-axi's agent-facing docs. Acts as an agent that has never seen the project, writes a review page for a given scenario from `--help` and `spec` alone, and reports where the docs failed it. Launch with a scenario that needs the changed component (without naming it), the repo path, and an output path outside the repo. ([MandalorianBatman/austere-axi](https://github.com/MandalorianBatman/austere-axi/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/cold-start-author.md) · ★8)
- **cold-start-beta-tester**: A working designer using Creative Companion for the first time with zero knowledge of how it was built — takes a client brief (usually from new-client-persona), tries to actually produce the deliverable through the platform, and reports every hole that stopped them. Drives the running app only; never reads the repo, the docs, or the source. Use to find what the platform is missing, as opposed to what is broken. ([nicholmahania-spec/creative-companion](https://github.com/nicholmahania-spec/creative-companion/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cold-start-beta-tester.md))
- **cold-start-path-auditor**: Simulates a cold-start agent entering through root AGENTS.md and verifies mandatory routing keystones are reachable. Weekly, and after any change to startup routing files. ([dzackgarza/research](https://github.com/dzackgarza/research/blob/HEAD/archives/provenance/lattice-research/agents/agents/cold-start-path-auditor.md))
- **cold-start-tester**: Fill the placeholders and pass the text below as the agent prompt. Write `{{SKILL_SUMMARY}}` in your own words; never quote the skill. - **cold-stranger-ux**: Reacts to the running local site AS A STRANGER who has never heard of the project, just got a text link from a friend, and has a 2-minute mobile attention span. Use when the user asks "what does a normal person think of this", "is this confusing", "audit the UX", or after meaningful UI/copy changes land on local dev. Does NOT read CLAUDE.md, TODO.md, or any project docs. Drives a real browser via Playwright at iPhone-14 viewport, takes screenshots, reacts in plain English. Use dentro da skill de-boa-para-excelente, na Fase 2, ou quando for preciso formular ou reformular o porco-espinho de um negócio a partir de material de entrevista já coletado. ([tamibot/creators-latam-starter](https://github.com/tamibot/creators-latam-starter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/comentarios-adicionales.md) · ★2)
- **comercial**: Agente comercial do EroErp 2.0, SOB DEMANDA — só quando o Eduardo pede (comando /divulgar). Produz material de divulgação do sistema: captura screenshots reais das telas (reusando a skill olhar-telas), escreve a copy e monta a arte (Canva/IA quando houver, senão HTML) no tamanho do canal, para o Eduardo revisar e postar. NUNCA publica, envia, agenda nem gasta — só entrega os arquivos e o texto. ([Eduardo-Ohlweiler/EroErp](https://github.com/Eduardo-Ohlweiler/EroErp/blob/HEAD/EroErp2.0/.claude/agents/comercial.md))
- **comercial-crm-implementer**: Implementador frontend del módulo comercial (clientes, leads, cotizaciones) para el proyecto Grupo Security. ([Soproyectos/grupo-security-office](https://github.com/Soproyectos/grupo-security-office/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/legacy-coordinacion-2026-09/kilo/agents/comercial-crm-implementer.md))
- **comercial-dev**: Gatekeeper de entrada a Kilo — valida issues y encamina trabajo de Excel a especialistas. ([Soproyectos/grupo-security-office](https://github.com/Soproyectos/grupo-security-office/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/legacy-coordinacion-2026-09/kilo/agents/comercial-dev.md))
- **comercial-tiendas-sin-cuota**: Atiende a un lead que quiere montar una tienda online sin cuota mensual, lo cualifica con preguntas concretas y redacta un presupuesto en euros listo para que el usuario lo revise antes de enviarlo. Se activa al recibir un mensaje nuevo de un posible cliente para este servicio. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/ecommerce-sin-cuota/agents/comercial-tiendas-sin-cuota.md))
- **comercializacion**: 1. **El alcance del vendedor es un filtro explícito de código, nunca una confianza en RLS.** Las server actions usan `createServiceClient()`, que **ignora RLS por completo**. La política `crm_prospects_owner_all` (`USING seller_id = auth.uid()`) protege las lecturas hechas con el cliente del usuario, pero **no** las de servicio. Por eso cada lectura pasa por `scopeForRole(role, userId)` y `applyCr ([VABUSTILLOS/Resurte.me](https://github.com/VABUSTILLOS/Resurte.me/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/comercializacion.md))
